- **Worker change (2026-10-23, P0 missing markers counted as 0; root not republished)**:
  - Live symptom: on `sg-graduate-employment.csv` the card "Median Gross Mthly 25 Percentile by School" ranked School of Law (only `na`/`N.A.`) at 0 and Yong Siew Toh at 1,500 (real median of reported values: 2,999).
  - Root cause: `compileAggregateExpression` in `frontend/services/duckdb/queryCompiler.ts` wrapped every single-column numeric aggregate in `COALESCE(<numeric>, 0)` (the old comment said it was for `'-` zero markers). Period-sum and unpivot aggregates did the same. The in-process executor already skipped these cells, so DuckDB and the fallback disagreed.
  - Fix: one shared `MISSING_VALUE_MARKERS` list (and `isMissingCellValue` / `buildMissingCellSqlCondition`) in `dimensionNormalization.ts`, extended with `n.a.`, `nil`, `nan`, en and em dash. Numeric expressions return NULL for blanks, markers and non-numeric text, and no aggregate coalesces to 0. COUNT(column) and COUNT DISTINCT skip markers, and a period sum is NULL only when every period is missing. The in-process executor, the Arquero fallback and the diagnostic templates follow the same rule. Valid `0` is unchanged.
  - Other demo files affected the same way: `sg-hdb-median-rent.csv` (2,273 `na` + 2,249 `-` in `median_rent`), `sg-public-transport-ridership.csv` (147 `na`). No footnote values such as `3200*` occur in the demo files.
  - Test: `frontend/tests/numericMissingMarkers.test.ts` (real CSV through intake → query engine → real in-process DuckDB; per-marker cases for DuckDB, Arquero and native). It failed before the fix.
  - Not changed: `utils/dataHelpers.ts` `parseNumericValue` still maps null to 0 for chart heights, zero-row hiding and the additive top-N sort. Non-additive ranked cards already sort NULL last through `rankRowsByValue`.
- **Worker change (2026-10-23, ranked-card fidelity; root not republished)**:
  - Found in a live gpt-5.4-mini run on `sg-graduate-employment.csv`: the card "lowest median 25th-percentile pay by school" was ordered ascending with LIMIT 20. `useAnalysisCardData` then re-sorted non-additive rows largest-first and kept 8, so the chart showed eight schools tied at 3,000 under "Top 8 view".
  - Fix: the card plan now carries `rankDirection` from the query's leading `orderBy` on the measure. The hook, `applyTopNWithOthers`, the scope labels and the executive KPI all follow it.
  - Small-n leaders: a COUNT companion query adds the value-gate code `small_group_leader` with a visible card caveat, and the card is caveated (`small_sample_leader`). An all-tied top-N window gets the code `tied_leading_values` and a note.
  - Tests: `tests/rankedCardPresentation.test.tsx` runs the real query and card path on the demo CSV, plus new cases in the topicProcessor, executiveKpis and analysisCardSummary tests.

## 2026-10-22 Singapore open-data demo datasets (data, catalog and test only; UI not wired)

- **Why**: give end users more than the HDB demo, with different table shapes and business themes for the AI analysis.
- **Change**: seven raw data.gov.sg CSVs under `demo-data/` with `<id>.manifest.json` each: `sg-graduate-employment` (MOE, d_3c55210de27fcccda2ed0c63fdd2b352), `sg-coe-bidding` (LTA, d_69b3380ad7e51aff3a7dcc84eba52b8a), `sg-business-formation` (SingStat/ACRA aggregate, d_739ef616852dcc1ecdb4a1c8bd0dcc8b), `sg-resident-unemployment` (MOM, d_79699e0bd51d2d8cee7fb3f6d29dc7c4), `sg-visitor-arrivals` (SingStat/STB, d_d1c33009b674dcf70b8e8c790b793f28), `sg-hdb-median-rent` (HDB, d_23000a00c52996c55106084ed0339566) and `sg-public-transport-ridership` (SingStat/LTA, d_ba615ec4cc5d9f5b7800ad82057f36f1). Six are byte-identical to the download; the median-rent file keeps only the latest 60 quarters (9,507 of 13,305 rows). `demo-data/catalog.json` (version 1) lists the two HDB demos first, then the seven, with four-language title/description/suggested question, theme, rows/columns/bytes, speed and source. Generator `scripts/make-sg-open-data-demos.mjs` (`npm run demo:sg:open-data`) owns the seven files, their manifests and the catalog text; manifests carry the source `lastUpdatedAt` instead of a run timestamp, so reruns are deterministic. Test `frontend/tests/demoDataCatalog.test.ts` checks the schema, languages, unique ids and that rows/columns/bytes (and manifest hashes) match the files.
- **Checks**: generator run twice with identical SHA-256 for all 15 outputs; new test 31/31; typecheck and lint pass.
- **Not done**: `FileUpload.tsx` does not read the catalog yet, and the new files are not in `SHA256SUMS.txt`/the Pages artifact until the next `npm run publish:root` (copied through `frontend/public/demo-data/`). `demo-data/README.md` still names `npm run demo:hdb:refresh`, which has no root npm script.

## 2026-10-22 E2E test run (no application code changed)

- **Static checks**: typecheck and lint baseline pass; publisher/stager tests 4/4. Full Vitest run: 430 files / 3,927 tests. The only failure, `duckDbSqlSecurity` WASM pin, was environmental: `frontend/public/duckdb/` is generated by `scripts/prepare-frontend-assets.mjs`. After running it, that file passed 32/32.
- **Local checked-in build (1.0.0-1a3e367fd880, Playwright/Chromium)**: home page loads with no console errors. Declining consent gives a local-only import of 4,988 rows. Data Explorer Preview (25 rows) and Aggregate-by-month (25/37 rows) run on DuckDB. At 390x844 there is no horizontal overflow. The service worker supports an offline reload, and History persists across reloads. The gateway is origin-bound, so a localhost `POST /demo/session` is blocked by CORS. The UI then shows "Unable to reliably analyze (0/9)" with Retry / Change provider, which is the correct graceful failure.
- **Live Pages (1.0.0-4ce484b0e78a)**: the HDB sample plus consent finished in about 170 s. Pi produced 2 cards and the page said "Results ready". There were 33 gateway calls, and none carried `max_output_tokens`. A 429 from the session cap refreshed the session and retried successfully. One 413 ("demo upstream request failed") made topic generation continue with empty topics. The headline totals match an independent recomputation (Model A 513.6M, Improved 439.9M, after case normalization of 27 raw labels to 18). The "56.9%" share uses the top-10 total as the denominator; the share of all models is 56.3%.
- **Findings**: (1) The 30 s activity timeout in `streamGenerateText` also counts time spent waiting on the cloud-AI consent dialog (consent runs in the provider middleware). If the user takes more than 30 s, intake structure detection and report-context extraction quietly fall back to deterministic logic. (2) Live Pages was last published 2026-10-08 (PR #27); the merged work after it is not deployed. (3) The 413 on the topic-generation request needs a gateway-log check. (4) Minor UX: on mobile the "AI Analysis" title is clipped by the header buttons. In local-only mode, the next-step CTA says "Review analysis evidence" even though no analysis ran.

## 2025-11-07 Session Notes

- **User Goal**: Review the current vanilla agent to ensure CSV ingestion works via `index.html` upload and `main_page.html` postMessage feeds, and diagnose AI preprocessing failures (`function did not return array`, header-mapping `includes` crash).
- **Key Observations**:
  - `processCsv` still emits `column_N` keys while canonical headers live in metadata/header mapping; AI transforms must call `_util.applyHeaderMapping`, otherwise lookups like `cleanedData[0][HEADER_MAPPING.column_2]` fail (`utils/dataProcessor.js` + `services/geminiService.js` prompts).
  - `executeJavaScriptDataTransform` only validates return shape after execution; there is no static lint to block functions with missing `return` or without `_util.applyHeaderMapping`, so malformed code repeatedly trips retries (`utils/dataProcessor.js:1172`).
  - Tool-first orchestration exists, but plans skip tools when `toolCalls` is empty, so repeated JS retries fall back to deterministic dataset without actually remediating the dirty CSV (`main.js:1887` onward).
  - `window.postMessage` bridge at `main.js:7047` trusts same-origin only; duplicates filtered via `signature`, so upstream sender must ensure identical payloads aren’t resent unintentionally.
  - Raw Data Explorer still shows report titles/embedded header rows (e.g., `KINETICS INDUSTRIES...`, `No / Payee Name / Amount ...`) because the deterministic `processCsv` step merely copies those rows into `structuredRows` unless `rowLooksLikeSummary` / `removeSummaryRows` flags them; current heuristics treat them as data rows after ingestion, so AI cleanup must explicitly drop them.
- **Latest Change**: Added deterministic leading/header row stripping inside `applyDeterministicPreprocessing` (see `main.js:3232+ removeContextualRows`) so title rows, duplicated header lines, and metadata-leading rows are removed before summary detection, with metadata counters (`removedContextRowCount`) and log entries for traceability.
- **Follow-up**: Raw data cleaning is now fully agent-driven. Stage plans/toolCalls are surfaced in the UI so users can verify each micro-step. `remove_leading_rows` pulls tokens from metadata + agent `keywords` args (no hardcoded list), and deterministic fallbacks only run when explicitly invoked by the LLM via tool calls.
- **Open Questions / TODO**:
  1. Should we auto-augment `dataForAnalysis.data` with canonical header aliases before invoking AI JS to reduce mapping errors?
  2. Would adding a guardrail that scans `jsFunctionBody` for `_util.applyHeaderMapping` (or forbidding `HEADER_MAPPING.column`) prevent the undefined `includes` crash?
  3. Need proposal for deterministic fallback steps when AI returns non-array; e.g., auto-run helper pipeline based on stage plan instead of skipping.
- **Pending Validation**: Confirm latest contextual-row heuristics remove the user-provided KINETICS dataset title/header rows in Raw Data Explorer; if still visible, extend fingerprint logic (e.g., ordinal prefixes).
- **Next Step**: Provide bilingual review + action options covering ingestion flow, prompt/pipeline gaps, and mitigation ideas for the reported errors.

  1. **Diagnose & Align Headers** – log dataset shape, call `detect_headers` + `remove_leading_rows` with metadata-derived keywords, refresh header mapping context, and append reasoning to chat log.

Crosstab flag was `false`, so no unpivot planned.

## 2025-11-07 Study Log (js validation warning not surfaced in UI)

- **Observation**: Later attempt log shows `Sanitized jsFunctionBody preview` with a huge `HEADER_MAPPING` mapping actual data values, followed by `function cleanAndReshape(...) { ... }` that *never returns anything at the top level*; it only defines the helper and returns from inside that helper. `services/geminiService.js:1850-1864` validates by executing `new Function('data','_util', body)` directly, so the absence of a top-level `return cleanAndReshape(data, _util);` makes the evaluation yield `undefined`, triggering `Transform function returned non-array` and `Generated function did not return an array.` This happens before orchestration hands control back to the UI, so the assistant panel never sees a warning unless we propagate `failureContext`.
- **Implication**: Model prompt needs to explicitly require top-level `return` statements (no nested helper-only definitions), and runtime should surface `failureContext.reason` into `addProgress`/agent log so users know why an attempt failed. Without this, validation loops silently, confusing users who only see the plan request spam.
- **Action Idea**: 1) Strengthen prompt schema to forbid helper-only bodies (or auto-wrap: if body defines `function cleanAndReshape`, append `return cleanAndReshape(data, _util);`). 2) When `lastError.failureContext` is set, push a progress/error log entry (e.g., `AI JS validation failed: Generated function did not return an array.`) so the assistant panel reflects the warning.

## 2025-11-07 Implementation (top-level return + surfaced warnings)

- **Prompt Update**: Added explicit rule in `services/geminiService.js` multi-pass instructions telling the LLM that any emitted `jsFunctionBody` must end with a top-level `return cleanAndReshape(data, _util);`-style statement—no helper-only definitions.
- **Sanitizer Guardrail**: `sanitizeJsFunctionBody` now auto-detects lone named functions without a top-level return and appends `return <name>(data, _util);` so validator/runtime always see an array-producing snippet.
- **Error Surfacing**: When JS validation fails, `failureContext.reason` is attached to the thrown error and `main.js` logs `AI JS 驗證失敗：<reason>，模型已重新規劃。`, ensuring assistant panel shows the warning instead of silently looping.

## 2025-11-07 Enforcement (toolCalls required + violation counter)

- **Schema/Prompt Enforcement**: `services/geminiService.js` now normalizes toolCalls, requires at least one deterministic tool invocation whenever `jsFunctionBody` is absent and status≠done, and the prompt explicitly states “no JS ⇒ toolCalls must be populated.” Plans missing both actions are rejected with `failureContext.type = 'missing_actions'`.
- **UI Feedback + Safeguard**: `main.js` now inspects `failureContext.type` to log either “AI JS 驗證失敗” or “計畫結構錯誤” and increments violation counters (`js_validation_error`, `missing_actions`). Two consecutive hits auto-trigger `enterAdjustPhase` + iteration budget extension, preventing infinite retries with empty plans.

## 2025-11-07 Header Refresh & Sample Normalisation

- **Prompt Input Fix**: `generateDataPreparationPlan` now pre-processes metadata via `ensurePlanMetadataHeaders`, scanning context/leading rows plus sample data to pick the highest-scoring header row (no硬編字詞). The resulting headers overwrite `inferredHeaders` before constructing the `HEADER_MAPPING`, so `column_1` finally maps to `Code/Description/...`.
- **Sample Slimming**: `normaliseSampleDataForPlan` only emits canonical keys (alias names take precedence; falls back to generic key when no alias exists). Duplicate `column_N` + canonical pairs are removed, reducing token noise and keeping the LLM focused on the tidy schema.
- **Net Effect**: Iteration 1 now receives both accurate header mapping and leaner sample rows, which should unblock deterministic tool calls (remove_leading_rows/detect_headers/remove_summary_rows) without relying on JS heuristics.

## 2025-11-07 Prompt Chunking (Diagnose snapshot → Tool plan → Optional JS)

- **README Alignment**: Documented the three-phase request flow (Diagnose snapshot, Tool plan, Optional JS) so future contributors understand why we chunk prompts instead of dumping whole CSVs.
- **Implementation**:
  - Added `formatDiagnoseSnapshot`, `selectPreviewColumns`, and `formatSamplePreview` so `generateDataPreparationPlan` now sends a tiny dataset summary plus 3-5 canonical rows instead of the full 20-row blob.
  - Updated `buildUserPrompt` to weave in the snapshot/sample blocks ahead of the stage instructions, while `formatMetadataContext` can skip context rows/sample previews when unnecessary.
  - Schema now requires `toolCalls`; if the LLM forgets, we auto-synthesize deterministic calls (remove leading → detect headers → remove summary) from the stage plan, ensuring each iteration actually runs a tool before retrying JS.

## 2025-11-08 Prompt Slimming & Syntax Fixes

- **Issue**: The plan prompt still concatenated 47-column schema + 20-row sample JSON blobs straight into a template literal and sprinkled unescaped backticks (e.g., `_util.parseNumber`), which triggered `Uncaught SyntaxError: unexpected token: identifier` in `services/geminiService.js` when the browser parsed the string.
- **Change**: Replaced the raw blob with a `leadBlock + instructionLines` workflow. Each guideline line now lives inside plain string arrays (double quotes), then `instructionsBlock = instructionLines.join('\n')` feeds the template via `${instructionsBlock}`. No more inline JSON dumps, no accidental backticks.
- **Snapshot Discipline**: Documented the 6-row cap for `contextRows` / `leadingRows` and the 5-row cap for `sampleDataRows` so future tweaks don’t reintroduce token bloat.
- **Verification**: Could not run `node --check services/geminiService.js` because the file uses ESM exports; run `node --input-type=module --check` or Vite build if we need static syntax validation.

## 2025-11-08 Bugfix: metadataForPlan ReferenceError

- **Issue**: Upload flow crashed with `ReferenceError: metadataForPlan is not defined` (stack: `buildAnalysisPlanPrompt → generateAnalysisPlans → handleInitialAnalysis`). Root cause: `buildAnalysisPlanPrompt` referenced `metadataForPlan` without defining it; only other prompt builders perform `ensurePlanMetadataHeaders`.
- **Fix**: Inside `buildAnalysisPlanPrompt`, derive `metadataForPlan` via `ensurePlanMetadataHeaders(metadata, rawSampleRows)` before calling `formatMetadataContext`, so all analysis plans get consistent metadata context without runtime errors.
- **Follow-up**: Should add regression coverage (unit or smoke) ensuring every prompt helper either receives `metadataForPlan` as an argument or computes it locally before formatting context.

## 2025-11-08 Bugfix: Plan generator `lastError` ReferenceError

- **Issue**: `generateDataPreparationPlan` crashed every iteration with `ReferenceError: lastError is not defined`, so Diagnose → Plan loop stalled after tool planning attempts. Function lacked a working body after the prompt builder, so it never called Gemini/OpenAI nor normalized outputs.
- **Fix**:
  - Added normalization helpers (`normaliseDataPreparationPlan`, `normaliseAnalysisSteps`, etc.) plus a deterministic fallback tool sequence (`remove_leading_rows → detect_headers → remove_summary_rows`) when the LLM omits tool calls and JS.
  - Rebuilt `generateDataPreparationPlan` to build the user prompt, call OpenAI/Gemini with schema + raw capture, sanitize the response, and return a structured plan (explanation, analysisSteps, stagePlan, agentLog, toolCalls, jsFunctionBody, outputColumns, status).
  - Error handler now attaches raw responses for debugging, so upstream violations log meaningful failureContext instead of hard `ReferenceError`.
- **Impact**: Vanilla agent can progress past Plan phase again; empty toolCalls now auto-synth to deterministic helpers so Execute step always has work even when the LLM only narrates.

## 2025-11-08 Prep Debug Logging

- **Goal**: Give QA clearer console breadcrumbs when Plan iterations stall or JS transforms fail so logs can be copy/pasted for remote debugging.
- **Implementation** (`main.js`):
  - Added helper `logDataPrepDebug(event, details)` that prints `[CSV Agent][PrepDebug]` with session ID, iteration, attempt, tool counts, JS preview, etc., truncating raw responses to <=400 chars.
  - Emitted logs for plan requests/responses, malformed JSON, planner failures, tool execution summaries, tool-only iterations, JS start/success/failure.
- **Usage**: When testing locally, open DevTools console and search for `PrepDebug`. Share the snippets (they already redact large payloads) so we can reconstruct the planner state without full network traces.

## 2025-11-08 Guardrail: HEADER_MAPPING misuse

- **Issue**: LLM 會生成 `data[0][HEADER_MAPPING.column_1]` 這類寫法，導致 runtime `undefined.trim`（raw rows只有 `column_N` key，無 canonical key）。
- **Fix**: `services/geminiService.js` 的 `normaliseDataPreparationPlan` 會在 `sanitizeJsFunctionBody` 後呼叫 `enforceSafeHeaderMappingUsage`。凡匹配 pattern `data[..][HEADER_MAPPING.column_N]` 會主動丟出錯誤，並塞 `failureContext.type = 'hard_coded_structure'` / `reason` 提醒模型必須改用 raw column key + `_util.applyHeaderMapping`。
- **Effect**: Runtime 不再等 JS 執行才爆錯；Plan 階段立即回報違規，內建 violation handler 會重啟迭代並帶入錯誤訊息，節省來回嘗試。

## 2025-11-08 Diagnose Metrics Upgrade (Plan A Step 1)

- **Goal**: 讓 Diagnose 指標可計算、可追蹤，後續好套 headerConfidence/shapeScore gate。
- **Implementation**:
  - `utils/dataProcessor.js:1011+` 的 `profileData` 現在對每欄輸出 `fillRate`, `typeShares {numeric/text/date}`, `nonEmptyCount/totalCount`，作為後續 header confidence 基礎。
  - `main.js:180-230` 新增 `computeHeaderQualityMetrics`、`computeShapeScoreFromMetadata` 等 helper，依照公式 `0.35*fill + 0.25*uniq + 0.2*structureMatch + 0.2*typeParse` 計算 `headerConfidence`，並把 breakdown 寫回 `metadata.structureEvidence`.
  - `evaluateHealthScores` 重構：`structureStability` 結合 headerConfidence、`reasonableness` 改用 `shapeScore`，`formatHealthScoreSummary` 會顯示 header%。
- **Impact**: Diagnose gate、Plan prompt 現在可取得真實 headerConfidence/shapeScore，後續步驟才能根據 minHeaderConfidence=0.7 這類條件做自動決策。

## 2025-11-08 Diagnose Metrics Upgrade (Plan A Step 2)

- **Profiler parity**: `profileData` 追加 `uniqRate = unique/nonEmpty`、`numericParseRate/dateParseRate/textParseRate`，metadata.columnProfiles 直接攜帶這些比例，Plan/Diagnose 無需二次計算。
- **Header evidence** (`main.js:198-374`):
  - `computeHeaderQualityMetrics` 現在輸出 `headerRegexHits`, `groupPatternHits`, `positionHint`, 以及 top-3 `weakColumns`（最低 fill/uniq）。
  - `metadata.structureEvidence.breakdown` 保存上述指標，UI/Plan prompt 可明確看到 headerConfidence 的計分來源。
- **Shape scores** (`utils/dataProcessor.js:457-530`):
  - `detectShapeTaxonomy` 回傳 `scores`（narrow/wide/crosstab/mixed）與 `shapeAmbiguous` flag。若前兩名差 < 0.08，標記 ambiguous，Plan 端可先重跑 detect_headers。
  - shapeScore 門檻：crosstab≥0.75、wide≥0.65、narrow≥0.55，否則 fallback 成 safer shape。
- **Plan context** (`main.js`, `services/geminiService.js`):
  - `buildPlanContextPayload` 統一傳遞 `headerConfidence/roleCoverage/shape/shapeScores` 至 Plan prompt（`Diagnose metrics` 區塊）。
  - `normaliseDataPreparationPlan` 強制 `shape∈{narrow,wide,crosstab,mixed}`、`expectedSchema.columns.length>0`、`toolCalls` 為陣列，違反直接拋 `invalid_plan`。
- **Structure evidence寫回**：`metadata.structureEvidence` 現在含 `headerConfidenceBreakdown`, `roleCoverage`, `shapeScore`, `weakColumns`，未來 UI 可直接渲染「診斷報告」。

## 2026-09-27 Direction Change: Keep React; Explore Browser Pi Harness

- **User decision**: Keep the React version and remove the separate Vanilla implementation. Another AI agent is performing that cleanup. This supersedes the earlier Vanilla-conversion goal recorded above; do not treat older README/roadmap/KB summaries as the current product direction until the other agent's changes are completed and verified.
- **Question**: Can this React CSV project integrate a framework-independent Pi-style harness that runs in the browser without a Node.js application server?
- **Assessment**: The full official Pi coding-agent CLI requires Node.js 22.19+ and terminal/system capabilities. The official `pi-agent-core` package documents browser app integration and separates Node-specific session backends; `pi-ai` documents browser support. A browser agent loop using Pi core, browser-safe CSV tools, and a React UI is feasible in principle. This is a documentation-based feasibility assessment, not a tested integration in this repository.
- **Runtime/build distinction**: No Node.js server is required for a statically hosted browser app, but the current React/Vite build and dependency bundling may still use Node.js during development/build. A strict no-Node-at-any-stage requirement would need a separately produced, pinned browser bundle or another build strategy and is not yet verified.
- **Boundaries to prove**: Browser tools can operate on user-selected files, in-memory/IndexedDB data, charts, and app state; the CLI's shell/filesystem/process tools are outside browser scope. Direct model API calls require browser-compatible provider access and expose user-supplied credentials to the browser; a backend proxy is needed if the application must hide its own keys. Keep one owner for each agent turn and preserve the CSV app's data/analysis responsibilities.
- **Current evidence / TODO**: Official Pi docs reviewed on 2026-09-27: `earendil-works/pi` coding-agent README, agent-core README, and pi-ai README. Current working tree shows another agent's React-preservation/Vanilla-removal edits in progress; their completion and runtime behavior were not checked here. Recommended next technical step after that cleanup: a small browser-only Pi core spike with one read-only CSV tool, event log, and direct-provider or proxy decision.
- **KB memory**: Saved the user-confirmed direction as a project-scoped semantic decision. The KB preview surfaced similarly worded memories for the different `React-CSV-Data-Analysis-Agent` repository; explicit repository identity resolved that false conflict. The current project's React cleanup remains in progress and was not marked complete.

The visible HDB columns look like an ordinary row table, making a detector mismatch plausible, but the exact shape-detector branch for this run was not exposed in the read-only UI. No browser actions, code changes, or publication were performed; only this local context note was appended.
- **Product clarification**: A standard row-table CSV should not require every end user to manually assign field roles or confirm structure. Human review is intended for genuinely ambiguous table boundaries or unsafe transformations. The HDB modal is an observed automatic-verification interruption, plausibly a false positive; its exact underlying detector branch still needs targeted reproduction before a code-level root cause can be asserted.

## 2026-09-28 HDB Structure Detection Fix

- **Reproduction and root cause**: A regression test loaded the public HDB CSV's first 2,000 records through the production dialect and intake pipeline. Its parser boundary was correct (header row 0, body row 1), but shape detection misclassified the first transaction record as a wide-table header with detail labels `IMPROVED`, `1977`, and `9000`. The downstream `wide_reshape_contract_complete` signal then failed and forced human review. The detector's flat-schema shortcut recognized human-readable headers with spaces or metric punctuation but omitted common descriptive `snake_case` headers.
- **Change**: The detector now recognizes descriptive lowercase `snake_case` column labels as a parsed flat schema when they make up at least half of five or more columns, while existing explicit header-layer detection remains in place. The HDB regression expects `already_tabular`, passing shape verification, and automatic analysis eligibility. Existing wide and complex report cases are retained as regression coverage.
- **Verification**: The new HDB regression and adjacent report-shape/structure tests passed (34 tests total). The full frontend suite passed (362 files, 3,409 tests), as did typecheck, lint, `publish:root`, and publisher/stager tests. A separate Playwright browser loaded the newly built app with all 982,589 HDB records, proceeded through automatic analysis 9/9 without a structure repair dialog, and produced 2 Verified cards plus 1 Needs review card. The app reported read-only large-file mode and 3 data-quality warnings.
- **Secondary E2E finding**: The investigation harness attempted `RowRole` and `SectionLabel` SQL diagnostics against the raw DuckDB-backed table, which lacks these canonical-only virtual columns; the browser logged one error and related warnings. Investigation continued and generated verified cards, so this did not block the HDB auto-structure outcome. Treat it as a separate schema-binding issue rather than claiming zero-console-error E2E.
- **Release verification**: Fix commit `c6e471a592c061f6ef8dd4921df6c2e14e3fbc6d` was pushed to public `origin/main`. Pages workflow run `36369540585` completed both stage and deploy jobs successfully. The live Pages `index.html` and app-agent bundle returned bytes with SHA-256 hashes matching the committed local files. The independent Playwright browser and temporary HTTP server were closed, and its task-created logs were removed.

## 2026-09-28 Backed Dataset SQL Column Alignment

- **Observed failure**: In full HDB E2E, the investigation harness queried `RowRole` and `SectionLabel` from the raw DuckDB file table, even though those fields only existed in the canonical preview/profile layer. The table rejected the queries; analysis continued with a console error and warnings.
- **Fix approach**: File intake records the column names returned by the DuckDB preview as backing metadata. The analysis context filters queryable profiles to that physical schema for `duckdb_file` datasets, preserving a source column with a structural-looking name when genuinely present. Older backing state can use the raw preview as a fallback. Ordinary in-memory datasets retain their existing profiles. If no physical columns are known, the queryable set is empty to avoid issuing SQL against guessed fields.
- **Verification**: The targeted queryable-profile, investigation-harness, and backing tests passed, along with the file-intake assertion. The full frontend suite passed (363 files, 3,412 tests), as did typecheck, lint, and `publish:root`. A separate Playwright browser loaded all 982,589 HDB records and reached automatic analysis 9/9 with 2 Verified cards and 1 Needs review card. The console reported zero errors and no missing-column or failed-task messages; remaining warnings were slow DuckDB tasks, a non-blocking semantic annotation timeout, and expected data-quality notes. The app still labels the result “Analysis ready with limitations” because the large file is read-only and uncleaned.
- **Release verification**: Fix commit `eb91f98a83642fb05ab79ed6f9f52f52b887fa94` was pushed to public `origin/main`, and the remote ref matched. Pages workflow run `36379294712` completed stage and deploy successfully from that commit. The live Pages `index.html` and app-agent bundle returned SHA-256 hashes matching the committed files (`732a910d1c5d66bdf6928c15b2b539d94772c9fd41ced3f92946a0e4545be2ec` and `72b9ccf07186fd9ac2298f8b74d656fc38bb1b6dbb4a231d35b4a89979128aa1`). The user's existing in-app browser tab was not controlled; HDB E2E used a separate Playwright browser against the local build.

## 2026-09-28 Provider Failure Versus Structure Repair Label

- **User question / evidence**: Manual HDB runs sometimes exposed “Review and repair data structure.” The pasted console transcript used `gemini-3.5-flash-lite` and included an optional semantic annotation timeout, but did not show a definitive provider transport failure. A later read-only inspection of the user's existing browser tab showed a successful 9/9 run with 2 Verified cards and a “Generate Analyst Report” action.
- **Prior contract**: Pi's provider stream uses a 45-second timeout and zero stream retries, with no automatic cross-provider switch. A failed run preserves imported data and ends as failed or degraded depending on completed stages and trusted cards. The simple-results action previously mapped any absence of a trusted business conclusion to “Review and repair data structure,” even when that completion gate did not prove a structure defect.

## 2026-09-28 Analysis Recovery Action Routing

- **Decision**: The user chose Option A: provider failure should show retry and provider settings; actual structure review should retain its repair action; insufficient trusted evidence should lead to evidence review.
- **Root cause and change**: The simple-results action previously equated the completion gate's lack of trusted cards with a structure defect. It now checks the pipeline's explicit structure-review state and the Pi runtime's recorded provider-failure category. Terminal runs with zero cards no longer exit through an empty placeholder before the recovery action. Failure category is cleared on new imports and persisted/restored with session state. The cleaning failure banner is suppressed when the terminal issue is a provider failure, because the Pi analysis loop may stop before cleaning begins.

The terminal UI kept the imported rows, showed Retry analysis and Change provider, and did not show either structure repair or a cleaning failure banner. An earlier browser pass verified that Change provider opened Settings and Retry analysis issued a new provider request. These failure-path tests intentionally do not prove the live gateway is healthy.

## 2026-09-29 Pi Average Card Semantics and Mobile QA

- **Live finding**: The previously deployed 27-Town HDB average card displayed shares of summed Town averages, an `Others` sum of averages, and a combined average total. A 27-category combo chart was hard to read on mobile. Baselines are in `UI/avg-card-baseline-desktop.png` and `UI/avg-card-baseline-mobile.png`.
- **Fix**: Additive card presentation is limited to `sum`/`count`. Non-additive Top N contains only real groups; its legend, summary, controls, executive KPIs, and chart menu avoid part-to-whole claims. Dense average cards default to a horizontal bar chart with a scrollable All view and expanded PNG capture. SQL table-mode cards now retain the executed aggregate; older cards with missing metadata use a neutral unknown-aggregation summary. Average time trends retain area/line options.
- **Browser evidence**: A synthetic 135-row, 27-Town CSV completed initial analysis 9/9 in the in-app browser. Pi saved the requested average-price card. Top 8 showed expected averages 572,000 to 502,000 without false shares or `Others`; All showed 27 groups, and the 27-row table's transaction counts summed to 135. The final static build restored the saved card after the original CSV was reselected and ranked All groups highest-first. On 390px mobile, document width was 390px and the long chart region had 540px visible / 1,106px scroll height. Final screenshots: `UI/avg-card-after-desktop.png` and `UI/avg-card-after-mobile.png`.
- **Verification**: Full source suite passed 367 files / 3,441 tests after the final source changes; TypeScript, lint, and `git diff --check` passed. PNG/CSV export commands were exercised in the in-app browser, but no download-file event was returned, so exported artifacts were not independently inspected. `UI/qa-audit.md` records the scope and limits.

The public entry, service worker, and three key bundles returned HTTP 200 with hashes matching the committed files. A new public in-app browser tab completed the synthetic 135-row analysis 9/9 and Pi saved a 27-row average card; its All table again summed to 135 transactions. That run exposed a `count` legend decimal (`135.00`) and a table-only card that displayed a one-row chart first despite its description. The follow-up formats count legends as integers and opens table-only plans with the table visible and chart controls omitted.
- **Follow-up root cause and local verification**: The SQL card executor overwrote `defaultDataVisible` and `dataTableFirst` from the planner, leaving the table collapsed even after hiding its chart. It now carries those flags through to the card. A fresh synthetic 135-row run completed 9/9 in the local in-app browser: one-row SQL evidence opened as visible tables without chart controls, and Pi created a 27-Town count card whose legend used integer `5` per Town. The full suite passed 367 files / 3,445 tests, plus typecheck and lint. Screenshot: `UI/pi-count-and-table-first-local.png`.
- **Second public E2E follow-up**: Release `a988a40` reached `origin/main`; Pages run `36495032518` passed for the exact SHA, and `index.html`, service worker, entry, AnalysisPanel, and app-agent bundles matched local SHA-256. In a fresh public in-app browser tab the synthetic dataset completed 9/9, SQL one-row evidence opened as a table, and Pi saved a 27-Town count card with integer legend values. Switching to All showed all 27 groups and 135/135 rows but revealed an inaccurate persisted `"Others" hidden` badge. The summary now suppresses that badge when no Top N fold exists; regression test added.

## 2026-10-03 Review bug fixes (health cache, classifier timeout, column summary)

- **Provider health cache** (`frontend/services/ai/providerConfig.ts`): results are now keyed by provider/simpleModel/API key and guarded by a generation counter, so a check that was in flight during a settings change cannot write a stale `invalid_key` back. The 8 s timer is cleared. `handleSaveSettings` only invalidates the cache when provider, simple model, or key changed (report-template saves no longer trigger an extra LLM call).
- **Intent classifier** (`intentClassifier.ts`): timeouts are detected with `isProviderTimeoutError` (the real message is "timed out", not "timeout"), and the classification stream has its own `AbortController` so an idle timeout cancels the SSE stream.
- **Column summary** (`chatOrchestrator.ts`): names/tokens of 1–2 characters now need whole-token matches, so `ID`/`NO` no longer match "provide"/"know" and crowd out relevant columns.
- **Checks**: TypeScript, lint, full suite (369 files / 3,455 tests) passed; 3 regression tests added. `npm run publish:root` regenerated the root deployment. Not pushed; Pages workflow not dispatched.
- **Follow-up**: the `not_configured` provider-health message (chat orchestrator and chat slice) now uses the localized key `provider_health_not_configured` (English/Mandarin/Japanese/Malay/Spanish/French) instead of hardcoded English; root deployment regenerated.

## 2026-10-03 Pi card quality verdicts and compaction telemetry

- **Evidence verdicts for Pi cards** (`piFollowUpRuntimeService.ts`): after every turn that created cards (any outcome), the new cards get a persisted `autoAnalysisEvaluation` computed with `attachAutoAnalysisEvaluationToCards` over the full card set (existing cards keep their stored verdicts). Without it the trust model labelled every Pi card unverified (`quality_evaluation_missing`). Evaluation failure is non-blocking and emits a `PiFollowUpRuntime` silent failure.
- **Scope limits**: this reuses the automatic quality evaluator only. It does NOT run the topic-pipeline `evaluateEvidenceValue` gate (needs semantic understanding and evidence plans from the initial-analysis runtime), and quality governance is not passed, so the unclassified-share tolerance is not applied. Pi cards may therefore be labelled more conservatively than runner cards. No browser comparison has been done yet (Gate 1 still open).
- **Compaction (Bug 3)** (`piContextCompaction.ts`, `piProvider.ts`): a failed, unusable, or ineffective summary is reported through `emitSilentFailure` (`PiContextCompaction`, recoveryAction `uncompacted_history_used:<reason>`) and not retried until the estimated history grows by 5% of the context window, instead of re-summarizing on every provider turn. Wired into the follow-up and initial-analysis runtimes.
- **Checks**: TypeScript, lint, full suite (369 files / 3,460 tests) passed after one unrelated flaky `piBrowserLab` failure that passed alone and on a full rerun. New tests were verified to fail before the change. Root deployment regenerated.

## 2026-10-03 Evidence value gate for Pi batch cards

- **Decision (user-confirmed)**: only `batch_analysis` cards are gated; explicit card requests (`precise_card`/`chart_card`) keep the user's exact spec. `reject` deletes the card and the reply lists it with the gate detail (not counted as created); `table_only` keeps the card, stores the gate result, and the reply notes "evidence supports table presentation only".
- **Implementation**: new `frontend/services/agent/runtime/pi/piCardEvidenceGate.ts`. Because Pi saves the card inside `analysis.create_plan`, the gate runs after the fact: it rebuilds the evidence plan from the card's query trace (`state.queryHistory` via `provenance.queryEvidence.traceId`) and its result summary, uses `latestAnalysisSession.semanticUnderstanding`, and passes earlier non-rejected cards as `existingAcceptedOutputs` for duplicate detection. The result is stored on `card.evidenceValueGate`, which the automatic quality evaluator already turns into `value_gate_*` reason codes. `piFollowUpRuntimeService.ts` settles the gate once per turn (success and failure paths) before composing the reply.
- **Not gated (by design)**: if there is no semantic understanding (no initial analysis ran) or no trace, the card is kept with the automatic quality verdict only. Gate errors keep the card and emit a `PiCardEvidenceGate` silent failure. Harness/steering context and AI evidence evaluation are not used (the topic pipeline also skips AI evaluation).
- **Checks**: TypeScript, lint, full suite (371 files / 3,468 tests) passed, including an unmocked test where the real gate accepts a first card and rejects an identical second card. Root deployment regenerated. Still no real-browser Gate 1 comparison.

## 2026-10-03 piBrowserLab flaky test root cause

- `tests/piBrowserLab.test.tsx` failed intermittently in the full suite because the dialog dynamically imports `piBrowserAgent` on click and testing-library's default 1 s `waitFor` timeout is too short for a cold module load under CPU contention. Reproduced 4/4 under artificial CPU load ("Unable to find a label with the text of: Pi answer" after ~1.5–2.4 s); no app bug. The wait now allows 10 s; 4/4 passes under the same load. Test-only change, no deployment regeneration needed.

## 2026-10-07 Import card "View technical details" button layout

- `FileUpload.tsx` (importing state): the button wrapped to three lines and was squeezed into an oval beside the privacy note. The button is now `shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap` and the note takes `min-w-0 flex-1`, so the label stays on one line. Layout-only change; root deployment not regenerated yet (needs `npm run publish:root` with installed deps).

## 2026-10-07 Default provider moves to gateway Demo sessions

- **Why**: the embedded XOR-obfuscated `gw_*` key was effectively public and the gateway now answers `401 invalid api key`. The Default provider now uses short-lived, origin-bound `dmo_*` sessions instead.
- **Implementation**: `services/ai/demoGatewaySession.ts` (in-memory token, single-flight refresh, 60 s expiry margin, `DemoGatewayError` carrying `error.code`). `fetchDefaultGateway` (`browserProviderFetch.ts`) still strips `max_output_tokens`, now also injects `Authorization: Bearer dmo_*`, rewrites `model` to the session's alias, and on 401 or "demo session request limit reached" (429) refreshes once and replays. `DEFAULT_GATEWAY_BASE_URL` is `.../demo/v1` for both the AI SDK and Pi paths. `DEFAULT_GATEWAY_MODEL` stays a logical label for settings, UI and context planning; the wire model comes from the session. `dmo_*` is redacted in local diagnostics. `xorCipher` and its test were deleted.
- **Needs input before deploy**: the project id comes from build-time `VITE_DEFAULT_GATEWAY_PROJECT_ID`; no value is committed. The gateway project must have `agent_tools_enabled=true` and allow `demo-openai-mini`.
- **Not done yet**: root deployment (`npm run publish:root`) is NOT regenerated, so the checked-in bundle under `assets/` still contains the old key until it is rebuilt with the project id. The old `gw_*` key must be revoked by the gateway operator after the new build is live. No live check against the real gateway, so `DEMO_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED` risk for strict `json_schema` outputs and tool round trips are unverified.
- **Checks**: typecheck, lint, full suite (343 files / 2,819 tests) passed.

## 2026-10-07 Automatic PWA updates with a manual check button

- **Decision (user request)**: updates install automatically by default; users may also click a header button that shows the build version. This replaces the earlier "wait for explicit confirmation" policy, which left stale builds (and the old gateway key) running until users clicked the banner.
- **Logic** follows the maintainer KB's PWA update runbook (versioned worker, no-cache script revalidation, periodic `registration.update()`, waiting worker applied via `SKIP_WAITING`, reload only after `controllerchange`). `pwaManager.ts`: `updateViaCache: 'none'`, check every 30 min plus on visibility and reconnect, `checkForPwaUpdate()` (waits for an installing worker, reports `up_to_date | update_found | error`), `autoApplyPwaUpdate()` gated by `setPwaAutoUpdateGuard`. `usePwaAutoUpdate` sets the guard from the busy state (import, initial analysis, active turn, report/summary) and re-applies when the app becomes idle, so a reload never interrupts a CSV import. The existing banner stays as the fallback when an update is held.
- **UI**: `PwaUpdateButton` in the header shows `v1.0.0 · <build>` (from the worker's `GET_VERSION`), then Checking / Up to date / Updating / Check failed; disabled offline. New `pwa_check_*` translations in four languages.
- **Limits**: auto-apply reloads the page when idle, so unsaved in-memory state outside the busy signals can be lost; a loaded dataset is not treated as busy. The service worker itself is unchanged. GitHub Pages ignores `_headers`, so no-cache for `service-worker.js` relies on `updateViaCache: 'none'`. Not verified in a real browser against a published update.
- **Checks**: typecheck, lint, full suite (344 files / 2,829 tests) passed. Root deployment regenerated.

## 2026-10-07 Demo gateway rejects replayed reasoning `content`

- **Live finding**: after the Demo-session build shipped, the Pi agent's second request failed with `400 DEMO_FIELD_DISABLED: content is not allowed in reasoning items`. Pi replays each stored reasoning item verbatim (`JSON.parse(block.thinkingSignature)`), including streamed `content` and `status`.
- **Fix**: `sanitizeReasoningInput` in `browserProviderFetch.ts` reduces reasoning input items to `type`, `id`, `summary` and `encrypted_content` at the final request boundary (the same place `max_output_tokens` is stripped). Covered by a boundary test.
- **Still unverified against the live gateway**: other replayed item shapes (assistant `message` items with `status`/`id`/`annotations`, `function_call` `id`, array-valued `function_call_output.output`) may trigger further `DEMO_FIELD_DISABLED` / `DEMO_INPUT_INVALID` errors. Fix them one by one from the reported `error.code`, or ask the gateway operator for the exact accepted item schema.

## 2026-10-07 Importing screen redesign

- **Why**: the loading state had weak information hierarchy (a spinner, one status line, an unstructured privacy sentence and a "View technical details" pill), so users could not tell what was happening or whether their data was safe.
- **What changed**: new `components/ImportProgressCard.tsx` (used by `FileUpload.tsx` while `isBusy`): file icon and title, a Upload/Parse/Analyse/Ready stage tracker driven by `aiTaskStatus` and `cleaningRunStatus`, an indeterminate progress bar, the live `progressMessages` line, "Processed locally" and "AI protected" cards, and a collapsible Technical details panel (engine, file type, AI provider, link to diagnostics). Card is 768px wide. Header (`AppHeader.tsx`) now shows the loaded file name beside the title and moves the version/update button to a quieter spot at the far right. Provider label shared via `utils/cloudAiProviderLabel.ts`. New `import_*` translations in four languages.
- **Deliberately not built**: percentage, rows processed, ETA, memory and exact file size from the mockup. The import pipeline reports none of them, and inventing numbers would mislead. "Cancel import" is also not built because import has no cancel path. Both need pipeline changes (structured progress events from `fileOrchestrator`, an abort signal) and are the proposed next step.
- **Checks**: typecheck, lint, full suite passed; real-browser screenshot of the importing state and expanded details confirmed the layout.

## 2026-10-07 Demo gateway client contract (confirmed against the live gateway)

- **Source**: the gateway operator verified the current live behavior (project `github-pages`) and reported the accepted schema; the client now follows it.
- **Input sanitizer** (`services/ai/demoGatewayInputSanitizer.ts`, applied in `fetchDefaultGateway`): reasoning items replay only with `encrypted_content` (otherwise skipped, and the following function calls lose their `fc_` id so OpenAI does not demand the missing pair); `function_call` loses `namespace`; `function_call_output.output` is always a string (array parts are flattened, images become `[image omitted]`); replayed assistant `message` items keep only `type/role/id/phase` and `output_text` parts with `annotations: []` (`phase` only `commentary|final_answer`); `top_p` and `max_output_tokens` are never sent. Shapes without a confirmed rule pass through untouched.
- **Concurrency and limits** (`services/ai/demoGatewayConcurrency.ts`, `browserProviderFetch.ts`): at most 2 requests in flight per page (a slot is held until the response body is fully read, errors or is cancelled, with a 10-minute safety release so an unread body cannot deadlock the app). `DEMO_SESSION_REQUEST_LIMIT` and 401 refresh the session once and replay; `DEMO_SESSION_CONCURRENCY_LIMIT` waits (0.4 s, 1.2 s) and replays on the same session; the per-IP 429 (30 requests per 60 s, session creation included) is surfaced, not retried.
- **Known gaps**: a history that ends with an assistant message is only reported with a console warning, because rewriting a conversation is riskier than a rare upstream 400. `strict:true` tools are not forced: Pi sends `strict:false`, which the gateway accepts; enabling strict would require OpenAI strict-mode schemas for every tool. `reasoning.effort` is mapped from the app's off/low/medium/high, so `xhigh` is never sent.
- **Verification**: a real Pi multi-turn history (reasoning, assistant text with phase, tool call with namespace, tool result) was run through the actual Pi provider path with a mocked network; the wire payload matches the confirmed schema. Not yet verified against the live gateway from the browser.
- **Checks**: typecheck, lint, full suite (347 files / 2,852 tests) passed. Root deployment regenerated.

## 2026-10-07 Plain-language shared-gateway errors (Phase 1)

- **Why**: failures from the shared Demo gateway reached users as raw provider text such as `OpenAI API error (400): {"message":"content is not allowed in reasoning items","code":"DEMO_FIELD_DISABLED"}`, which says nothing about what to do next.
- **What**: `utils/gatewayErrorMessage.ts` classifies a raw error string into session, concurrency, IP rate limit, daily quota, unavailable, upstream, rejected request, or stale build (the retired private-key 401 that only an old cached build can still hit), and returns a localized message, a next step, and the gateway `error.code`. Unrecognised errors return null and keep their existing text. Applied where Pi failures reach users: the initial-analysis status card subtitle (`piInitialAnalysisRuntimeService.ts`) and the follow-up chat reply (`piFollowUpRuntimeService.ts`). Raw text is unchanged in the run outcome and diagnostics.
- **Not covered yet**: the AI SDK paths (intake detection, report-context extraction, connection test) still log raw errors because they fall back silently or have their own surfaces; the Settings "Test connection" message and the `Retry` / `Change provider` card were not changed. A "copy technical details" action is not built; the code in parentheses is the only technical hint.
- **Checks**: typecheck, lint, full suite (348 files / 2,865 tests) passed, including a runtime test that the initial-analysis subtitle is friendly while the outcome keeps the raw message. Root deployment regenerated.

- The version/update button (`PwaUpdateButton`) moved from the far right of the header to directly in front of History, so it is next to the other utility actions and visible on the upload page. Its checking / up to date / updating / failed states are unchanged.
- `PwaStatusBanner` no longer renders while an update is being applied: the header button already shows "Updating…", and the banner text ("Refresh when you are ready") contradicted an update that was already running. The banner now appears only while an update is held (for example during an import or analysis) and keeps its explicit refresh action.

## 2026-10-07 Analysis-in-progress screen: less noise, honest progress (UX review batch A)

- **Feedback**: users saw many warnings during an unfinished analysis, and the screen contradicted itself ("Full dataset loaded in read-only mode / No cleaning is applied" next to "Apply ... safe and reversible cleaning operations through the existing mutation and lineage owner"). The second line was the developer stage description from the tool manifest, shown as user copy.
- **Policy**: data-quality notes are handled by the analysis agent and need no user action, so they stay as one quiet collapsed line. Only two things deserve the user's attention: losing a loaded large file on refresh (kept, in one neutral line) and a failed analysis (error card with retry).
- **Changes**: `piInitialAnalysisRuntimeService.ts` now sends `titleKey`/`subtitleKey` (`analysis_initial_stage_{1-9}_title|desc`, four languages) instead of the manifest description; the manifest text is unchanged for the agent. `AiTaskStatusBubble` shows a 9-segment step progress bar (completed / active / waiting) and "N of M steps done · continues automatically". `AnalysisPanel` hides the "Results view" toggle while nothing exists yet and shows `AnalysisResultsSkeleton` (summary, key findings, charts placeholders); the large-dataset notice is neutral grey with consistent wording ("Your original CSV is never modified"); `DataQualityWarnings` is a collapsed one-line note with the details inside.
- **Honesty rules kept**: the bar is step-based (completed steps / total), not a time or row percentage; no row counts, ETA or sample-versus-full per stage are shown because the pipeline does not report them.
- **Not done yet (batch B)**: compact dataset header and removal of repeated file name / row count / "AI Analysis" chip; per-stage rows and sample-versus-full labelling; severity grading of quality notes; Cancel for the initial analysis; header de-cluttering and typography/max-width; named-step track (Import → Profile → …) labels. Not verified in a real browser against a live analysis run; covered by component and copy tests only.
- **Checks**: typecheck, lint, full suite passed; root deployment regenerated.

"Prepared rows" wording is replaced by plain "rows" (new key `report_header_meta_rows`). Low-confidence and fallback badges stay because they carry information. The collapsible details panel is unchanged.

- **Verified in a real browser** (Playwright, mocked gateway returning DEMO_UPSTREAM_HTTP_ERROR): compact header ("30,000 rows · mid.csv") and the plain-language failure message with its code render as designed. The in-progress screen itself could not be reproduced because the first stage status only appears after the model's first tool call; it is covered by component tests only.

## 2026-10-07 Report title no longer shows the column names

- **Problem**: for a CSV with no title lines the dataset header displayed the column names joined together ("month town flat_type resale_price") as if it were the report title. It comes from the schema fallback in `services/agent/reportContext.ts` (`inferFallbackReportTitle`), which is deliberate and pinned by a test for the AI context.
- **Decision**: the data/AI context is unchanged (the joined column names remain useful prompt context and the existing test still passes); only what the user sees changes. `isSchemaDerivedReportTitle(title, columnNames)` detects a title made only of the dataset's own column words (joined, reordered or partial; a single word never counts), and `ReportHeader` skips such candidates, so the file name is shown. `AnalysisPanel` passes `columnNames` from the first row of the prepared data.
- **Limit**: a real title made only of column words (for example "Sales Region" for columns Region and Sales) is treated as schema-derived and replaced by the file name. That is the safer failure.
- **Verified** in a real browser (Playwright) with a plain 30,000-row CSV: the header shows "mid.csv". Typecheck, lint, full suite passed; root deployment regenerated.

## 2026-10-07 Layout plan phases 1 and 2: fewer limitation notices, readable numbers

- **Source**: live screenshots of a full 982,589-row run (all nine stages, charts, KPIs) showed three stacked "limitations" messages, a yellow "Dataset cleaning is currently running" banner during the analysis, the step tracker disappearing at stage 8, technical wording ("DuckDB") and unreadable numbers (axis 80,000,000,000; KPI 297,838,107,220).

It lives in its own module so tests that mock `agentMonitor` are unaffected. (b) `AnalysisStatusSection` hides a finished ("done") status line once cards or a final summary exist. (c) The cleaning banner is not shown while the initial analysis is running. (d) Copy: the degraded next-step text no longer repeats "limitations" ("Ready to share. The report keeps the visible evidence and notes what could not be verified.") and the stage summary no longer mentions DuckDB.
- **Phase 2**: `utils/compactNumber.ts`; chart value-axis ticks use `formatAxisValue` (compact from 10,000: 80B, 20K) in bar, horizontal bar, stacked, multi-line and common configs; tooltips and tables keep exact values. Headline KPIs use compact form from one million (297.8B); `buildTotalMetricLabel` no longer yields "Total Sum …". No currency symbol is added because the dataset carries no currency information.

Axis and KPI abbreviations were verified by unit tests only, not in a real browser run.
